2a1(da): @##/gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs gsang ba'i ye shes/don gyi snying po rdo rje bkod pa'i rgyud/rnal
2a2(da): 'byor grub pa'i lung /kun 'dus rigs pa'i mdo/theg pa chen po/mngon par rtogs pa/chos kyi ma grangs rnam par bkod
2a3(da): bA zhes bya ba'i mdo/bam po thog mar zug pa'o//bcom ldan 'das dpal sgrol byed thum
2a4(da): rje'i rgyam pa'i rgyal po shin tu rnam par grol ba nyid la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
2b1(da): cad kyis bcom ldan 'das gsang ba pa'i bdag po rdo rje 'dzin pa chen po//ji skad du rnam par bgro ba'i skyes bu/smra ba'i seng+ge de/'di ltar sang+yas kyi zhing bsam
2b2(da): gyis mi khyab na 'khod cing /sems can gyi rigs tha dad pa na bzhugs te/bsdam gyis mi khyab bsam du med po'i chos/dpag tu med pa'i rgyal ba kun gyis bsngags pa'i
2b3(da): 'du bral med pa'i gzungs thams cad rig pa/gsang sngags kyi ngo bo//ji snyed pa'i ji bzhin pa/chos thams cad kyi me long de nyid/mi 'gal bar nyan cing //
2b4(da): ma nor bar ston te/rnam par bgro ba ni 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas mi bskyong po'i zhing khams mngon par dga' ba zhes bya ba/rdo rje'i 'od zer gyis rnam par brgyan pa na/
2b5(da): rdo rje phung po'i ri bo la/skyes bu chen po'i sems dpa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba drag sa ya'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'ad gsal
3a1(da): da du babyung ba'i skyo nas/sa par 'gro bar byed do//sAngasu rgyas rin chen 'byung ba'i zhing khams dpal dang ldan pa zhes bya ba/rin pa che'i phung po rnam par bkod pa na/
3a2(da): bai rgya phyi'i 'ang mtho ba zhes bya ba'i ri bo la/skyes bu chen brdo rje rin tshon zhes b+hA bA/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba breg sa yo'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi 'od gas
3a3(da): rnas par 'gro bar byed do/sangs rgyas 'a yu ga sa pa'i skyed khams/ bcan nas byug mengs par daklong pa
3a4(da): la bya /skyes nag sa kyi b+h+yabya chub sems dbu' bye thugs pa nga
3a5(da): dkyi 'phe 'gasal pa/gsang sngags kyang ba cid/rang gi sbyin pa la ba cig rang gi so long pa bzhin rna ma ru mi kyi
3b1(da): sar banang pa rnasarnam par 'gro bar byed pa sas rgyas da mas par
3b2(da): mas rnam par dag par shes bya bar/in+d+ra ni la'i dra bas brgyan pa nA/rin pa ni tshag
3b3(da): bkod pa'i sgra zhes bya ba'i ri bo la/skyes bu chen po rdo rje las zhes bya bar/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag sa ya'i 'khor dang bcas
3b4(da): sems kyi dkyil 'a khor 'du gsal ga/gsang sngags snang ba de nyid/rang gi stan bral nyan cing /rang gnyi 'khor la de bzhin
3b5(da): mings su 'das nas kya man par brtags pa'i gnasam par gro bar gyi pa/sras rgyas rnam par sa
4a1(da): gzhing khams/byang chub kyi snying po can zhes bya ba/sems sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa na/chos kyis mtho ba zhes bya ba'i ri bo la/skyes bu chen po rdo rje 'dzin pa zhes pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' bye ba/
4a2(da): phrag sa ya'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi rang bzhin 'od gsal ba/gasdang sngags kyi ngo bo de nyid/rang gi ston pa la nyan cing /rang gi nye 'khor la de bzhin ston to/yongasu 'das nas kyang /de kho na nyid du nges pa'i sgo nas/de rnam par
4a3(da): bgro bar bya'o//sangs rgyas thub pa'i dbang po zhing khams/mos pa can mi mjed kyi 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu zhes bya bar/rdo rje gdan gyis rnam par brgyan pa/gnod sbyin gyi gnas pa lcang lo can gyi pho brang na/skyes bu chen po gsang ba pa'i/
4a4(da): bdag po zhes bya bar/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag sa ya/gnod sbyin gyi rigs can mang po'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi rang bzhin 'od gsal ba gsang sngags gnas pa lcang lo can gyi pho brang na/rang gi nye 'khor la de bzhin bston to//de bzhin du sang+ga
4a5(da): rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i zhing khams dpag tu med pa na/grangs med pa'i skyes bu chen po zhes bya bar/byang chub sems dpa' bye phrag sa ya brjod kyi mi lang ba'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi rang bzhin 'od gsal ba/gsang sngags kyi ngo bo mtha' yas pa de nyid/
4a6(da): rabang gi nyen 'khor dag la de bzhin ston te/yongasu 'das nas kyang /nges par brtags pa'i sgronas/de rnam par 'gro bar bye//de bzhin du chos kyi sku'i zhing khams/snying po byang chub zhes bya ban/rgyal chub skyes bu zhes bya bar/rgyal tshab skyes bu bsam gyis mi khyab
4a7(da): pa'i 'khor dang bcas te/sems kyi ring bzhin lenas sangs par gyur ba/gsang sngags kyi dadzo nyid li/dgos khengs par 'grel to//de bzhin du sems can gyi rigs pas sa gyis mi khyad po/lha'i cis dang /lha mo yin kyangs dang /klu sa dang /ma'i ris dang /
4b1(da): gnod sbyin gyis dang /srin po'i sa dang /byol song gi'is dang /yi dgas kyi sa dag dmyal ba'i ris la sogs pa/'dzig rten bu'i phyos cu'i thadadapam dang thon dnga'i skyes bur gzugs pa dang par snang ba dag /rang gi sa kyi 'dor ba dang ba dang bcas te/sems /
4b2(da): gyim long logs pag gsang sngags kyi bo nyid/rang gi thub pa'i dang po la/ra rang gi skang dang /b.rang rung /mos pa sna tshogs kyis nyan cing /ming du mtshan mar brtags pa'i snonas/rang nga ni nye 'khor la debanan ston to//daN+Da d+hAng+ge bya gub sems dpa
4b3(da): /de dag kyang /la la ni byang chub kyi ris can dag/la la ni byang chub pa dag/la la ni byang chub sems dpa' dgo/la la ni byang chub sems dpa' dag go//la la ni byang chub chen po dgo//de bzhin du bzhi lnga nas btags pa/tha dad du grags pa ni 'di lta ste/rnaM su yi rangs pa
4b4(da): dag/dang /ma skyes pa'i cho sa la bzod pa thob pa dag dang /chos laM chos kyi mig rdul med cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam par dag pa dang /phyir mi ldog pa'i sa la gnas pa dag dang /lung bstan pa thob pa dag dang /'dun pas phyogs pa dang /ngang pas mos pa dag dang
4b5(da): yongasu bsti gnas pa dag dang /skye ba gcig gis thogs pa dgra dang /srid pa tha ma dag dang /lam du 'jug pa dang /'gro ba dang ngag dang /phyin pa dag dang /phyir 'ongs pa dag dang /gnas pa drag dang /smon pa dag dang /ldang ba dang /'jug pa dang /gnas pa dang /
4b6(da): rdzogs pa dang /brtsol ba pa dang /slob pa dang /mi slob pa dang /goms pa pa dang /mngon du gyur po pa dang /rgyud du byung ba bya dang /rigasungs pa dang /cho su shes pa dang /chos lags pa dang /rgyal tshab pa dang /rigs thams cad pa dang /gzungs sngags pa dang /rigs
4b7(da): sngags pa dang /gsang sngags pa dang /rigasum bzhi pa lnga pa dang /tshogs pa pa dang /sbyor bi ba dang /mthong ba pa do //bsgom pa pa dang /mthar phyin pa pa dang /'dun pa'i rnal 'byor pa dang /rigs chen 'byed pa'i rnal 'byor pa dang /dbugs chen 'byin pa'i rnal gyi
5a1(da): /pa dang /lung chen thob pa'i rnal 'byor bapa dang /rgyal chen rdzogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa dang /tshogs gi tshogs pa dang /yi ge'i tshogs pa dang /ming gi tshogs pa dang /brda'i tshogs pa dang /tha snyad kyi tshogs pa la sogs pa sha dgu go//de dag las bani/
5a2(da): gsum pa ni gsang sngags kyi ngo bo nyid la 'jug/spyod pa dang /gnas pa dang /thob pa dang /phyir lta ba dang /yong su rdzogs pa'o//de kun tu sbrel gnas pa dang /sa la gnas pa dang /tshol gnas pa dang /phyag rgya chen po dang /lhun gyis grub par gyur pa 'dra' zhig/de ltar na 'jig
5a3(da): rten gyi khams tha dad pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 'dzam bu gling tha dad na/bye ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu dang bcas pa mtha' yas par/'jig rten gyi sgron me byed pa mtha' yas par shar ba dadag kyang /'khor ba thog ma tha ma med pa brjod kyis
5a4(da): nga ba'i khams/skal pa thog mo tha ma med pa brjod kyis me la ngo ba'i skabs la/rtog par rgyan gyi khor mor 'byung zhing /sgron ma'i bya bas/lha mi 'jig rten gyi mun pa gsol nas/sems kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar byas te/nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor nub pa ltar yongs su
5a5(da): myangan las 'da'o zhes/thams cad nas thams cad du grags shing rnam par snang ngo ///sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo chen po las/thun mong bca' gzhi'i le'u ste/dang po'o///de nas ji ltar nga ltar 'di ltar mi mjed ces bya ste/
5a6(da): 'jig rten gyi khams na bzhugs pa'i ston pa rdo rje thub pa'i dbang po de/sku rgu'i tshe lo brgya pa la byung ste/yun hu cang mi ring ba zhig bzhugs te/yongasu mya ngan las 'da' bar dgongs na/yul yangs pa zhes bya bar 'khor mang por dang /thabs cig tu bzhugs par dbyar gyi
5a7(da): ngogasum 'as nas/'jig rten gyi slob ma la gangs dag gsum bzhag pa'i sladu/'khor 'dus pa rnams kyi mngam/rdzu 'phrul cher spyod la bka' stsal pa/rdzu 'phrul cam myong ba 'jig rten drug gi phyogs btsun/ba slob ma gang dag 'bong ba//'di 'i phyogs pa gang ga
5b1(da): ji steng ba rnams spras shig/nges par bzhag par bya ba dag yod do ces gsus pa dang /rdzu 'phrul thams chos kyi skang cig gis go bar bsgrags nas/thams cang bsdus pa ni ji lta ste/rgyal ba'i ni gyu sa drug gang sung sa/'di ba ngas shing phyogs bkun nyon cig/
5b2(da): /'dir rgyal ba bdud rtsi gsum 'jog gis/dug gsum 'joms par 'dod pa kun 'du shig/di skad ces bsgrags bla/dam pa'i rigs can lnga me gtogs ni 'di lta ste/sngags kyi bsam gtan chen po'i nang du yang dag 'jog pa/bskul ba chen po lang ka'i mnga' bdag/blo gros
5b3(da): /thabs ldan la sogs pa las gzhan thams cad 'dus par gyur to//de nas 'dus pa de dag la bka' stsal pa/kye grogs po ngas shes par gyis shig/chos thams cad ni mi brtag pa'o//'byor pa thams cad ni mthar chung ba'o//dngos po thams cad ni nges par 'jig pa'o//
5b4(da): gang zag gi srog gi dbang po la skye shi yod pa'i phyir/yongs mya ngan las 'da' bas/su dag the tshom mchod pa rnams dri ba gyis shig/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /'khor der 'dus par gyur pa'i rigs 'dzin rgyal por gyur pa kun dza ra zhes bya ba de rang gi stan las langs te/
5b5(da): seng ge'i lha stengs kyi ston pa'i zhol la bltas nas/de skad ces gsol to//khyod ni na mkha'i rang bzhin te/'da' dang mi 'dar yongasu me mnga'/'jig rten pa la mthun 'jug tu/'da' bas yongasu 'dul bar dgongs/khyod ni 'jig rten sgron mar shar//lha mi 'jig rten thams
5b6(da): cad kyi tsa munu pa'i dkyil 'khor kun gsal nas//sems kyi dkyil 'khor gsal mdzad//sra dang rnam thar la spyad pas//byang chub thob par 'gyur ba'i//'dren pa'i theg pa rnam gsum la//gang dag rigs par bkod lags na//mnyam pas blang dor med spyod cing //byang chub
5b7(da): gzhan nas mtshol ba'i//thang sa mchog dpag gis mi lang ba'i//nges pa'i zug chen yongs ma gsungs/rgyal bar dag sa 'a chi gsum po//gdams ngag lung dum gan na//blo chung mun pas mongs pa rnams/nges pa'i bu gros gang la bzhag rnam par thar brjo
6a1(da): sa ba shA ga / gas gzu / mag smra ba rgyug/byu pa na po b+h+'u ga/no ba ga char gcod chug/ga sog sa
6a2(da): rnarga sa ga/'a stsal gcig pa na chen pa ga so/khyung ng+yas pa ni sa tulagag/ d+hi g+h+na ga pa slo ba sta rnal / bcu gyu hU~M/bang tshus pa gtsug sag pan yang byung //kha sa/
6a3(da): gyi sgron ma yo su bAn yang d+hA//gang gi sa pa bskyed pa na yang byung/ste byu chub nyid du sa pan snang byung / sa pas pa sgrib ba nas byung / nga gyi bangs pa dang 'i che yi byung /byin gyi rnyings las snang banas drung /rgyung ba zangs kyis
6a4(da): grags pan sa /gang tshang du mi ban yang byung chung sa pa sbyin pa na sang byung /yes rgyud na chu rtsong ngan ma zung /rmongs pa rna por ga yo pang byung / ga thas di khom cams thams cang du/kun rgyal gyi rnam pa
6a5(da): dang su /nga //zhes gsasu tshung cu yi // nga sa /nga du gyur/khrong nga gyu pas spyo/ma 'a
6a6(da): gyi chu zhigagi kyang // 'i tshul ga pa'i ro mu sa /sa gi sa da pa rdos su sa
6b1(da): las sadang gsug dang pogs pa byung gsum ga/'a nas yig 'i tshogs nyi pa'o//ma chung nges pa'i blo yong byed//khrig pa ris par zhin de//bsgru la chu kyi//rnam pa gnyis kyi bya ba bas//'jug pa dul yang brtson//gab rjod/
6b2(da): pade nyid du do//zhes bka' stsal bas/dri ba gsum pa'i yongasu rtog pa chang ngo //de nas yang stonas dri ba bzhi pa dang lnga pa tshig le'u gcig pa bka' su la ga/gzhan yang dri ba tha ma gnyis//gcig gis yongasu chod par byas//chos kyi spun du byas pa'i//rig pa
6b3(da): 'dzin pa khyod nyon cig/sgron ma byed pa'i bya bA yang /sngon du byung bar lung bstan pa'i//'gro ba ma lus kyi sems dpa' dag/byung nas gsal byed logasun 'byin//du skad ces bka stsal pA dang //d+hi ba bAzhi pa dang lnga ba'i yongs rtog pa tshang /yongasu mya ngan las
6b4(da): 'das por gyur to//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo chen po las/gdam sna ngagrangs par bzhag bI'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de'i tshe de dus na gang dag mi chen po rnams ni/yongasu gdung ba med par nang du yang dag 'jog/byis pa rnams
6b5(da): ni yo su gang du bar gyur te/ la ye bor gyur ning shin tu blo nas rtag par rab tu //'jig rten pa la la ni sha pa 'jug par gyur/la la ni brgyal zhing 'bog par gyur/la la mi khams nas rongam po bzhin du gyur to//de bzhin du thams cad kyang rgyo
6b6(da): mang po pa shi ba zhan du/kye hud kye dung zhes gdung gi'i sgra sgrog so//lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten yang /snod ni yong su mi 'ong bar gyur to//bcud kyang sems kyi dkyil 'khor mog mog por gyur to//chos 'di bar phyogs pa rnams ni mdzes nag por gyur tong
6b7(da): bdud kyi cha 'dzin cing gzhan gyi sde rnams ni/gzhan gyi dkyil 'khor dkar por gyur te/kun kyang bcas ka'i tshig sgog par gyur to//de nas lo brgya dang bun gnyis 'das pa na/dam pa'i rigs can sems dpa' chen po dag/nang du yang dag 'jog las langs pa dang /
7a1(da): lijig rten gyi ston banu par gyur pas/mgachang bar su nas kun kyang ma rang gi mngon par shes pas/lang ka'i yul gyi grong brdal gyi mtsho gling /rim pa yi gnam lcags yod pa'i rtsel rdzu phru kyi bya bas mngon par h+mA/
7a2(da): tu 'duso//yongasu 'dus nas 'dus pa de kun kyang /mgrin gcig tu shin tu nyams thag pa'i nga ros//smre sngags kyi gdung ba gton pa ni 'di lta ste/kye ma'o kye hud e ma'o klong //ston pa'i sgron ma'i 'od nub na//'jig rten mun par gang gis sil//tshad ma'i zhal de mi
7a3(da): bzhugs pas/tshad ma gsungs pa bstan pa la//'jug par 'tshal ba 'gro ba rnams//log par 'jug par mi 'gyur ram//tshad mar dam bcas gang zag gis//nges pa rnyid pa'i gong zag la//phan par bya phyir bka' bkrol ba//gtan la phebs par 'gyur zhig
7a4(da): legs nyes byed pa'i spyan bum pas/bka' nyid khyad du bsad pa'i skon gyi spyi gtsug mthar phyin pas//log stobs 'phel bar mi 'gyur ram//log pa'i bstan pa'i sprin 'khrigs pas//bden pa gnyis kyis nyi zla nub//'gro ba gang zhig lam tshol ba//mun par
7a5(da): 'khyams par mi 'gyur ram//ston pa mya ngan 'das 'og tu//bdud dang srin po mu stegs dang //gnod sbyin 'byung po mi ma yin//ma rungs gdug pa sus 'dul 'gyur//dri med bstan pa'i bdud rtsi 'di//nub na shin tu mi rung ni//rtsa la dang ldan pa su zhig gis//'bar zhing
7a6(da): snang bar byed par 'gyur//bslab pa dam pa rin po che//bral na shin tu mi rungs na/skyes bu dam pa gang zhig gis//tshul rnams 'dzin pa byed par 'gyur//ston pa mi bzhugs de rjes nas//ston pa'i slob ma ji snyed pa//'phags dang gang zag tha dad pa//lung bstan
7a7(da): 'byung ba gang du zhig/bstan pa na mkha' 'dra ba la//mos pa tha dad par bzung nas//yin dang ma yin cha 'dzin cing //brtsod pa 'phel bar mi 'gyur ram//de nyid nges par rigs gcig la//lugs mi mthun par tha dad spyod//rigs gnyis tha dad rigs rnams su/
7b1(da): bslad cing bcos par mi 'gyur ram//rin thang med pa'i rin po che//mi mkhas gang zag tha dad bla/nyon mongs 'dod pa'i khrid dbang gis//dug dang rje bar mi 'gyur ram//chos kyi rgyal po ston pa de//'di sngon rkyod zhal bzhugs te//ma rungs pa rnams tshe bgyid na
7b2(da): gdug ba ngar bar mi 'gyur ram//gang 'dir bstan pa gnas pa'i yun//ji srid cig tu gnas par 'gyur//gang 'dir sngar byung gang sngar byung //phyis 'byung dus 'di ji ltar 'gyur//ston pa'i bstan pa tha ma'i tshe//nges par ji ltar 'gyur zhig gu/bslab pa 'dzin
7b3(da): 'i slob ma rnams//the ma dzi b+hU bAm+b+haR+tha zhig/'di nas mya ngan 'das 'og tutA/gang lta su yi ston par gshegs/zhing 'ams phyogs ni gdan bzhugs//den bstan pa ci zhig sgrogs//nga yong 'jig rten mgon mang rnams//mgon dang sgyu suM gang zhig 'a
7b4(da): 'phrul gyi blo dang gros stor na//blo dang gis ni gang nas btsa lo//des pa'i don 'di ma d+hA nga naM//de yo dis don gang la bzung //mchod gnas yid rten mi bzhugs na//sku gdung ji ltar 'gyur ni gu/klud kyis mchod gnas tshil bgyi//nga ltar ston po 'das 'og tu//gdung
7b5(da): 'tsho ba skyes bu 'byung 'gyur ram//kye ma'o kyi hud a ma klong //zhes yongasu gdung ba bton to//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo chen po las//thog mar dam pas gdung ba bton pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de skad ces smras
7b6(da): gdungaso babs//srid gsun 'gro ba'i gtan gcig po//rgyul ba rgyam tsho rnams kyis gsan//snying rdzi'i bdag pong dag kun//lpa po can gyi pho drang du//gsang ba'i bdag po'i gan su 'dus//bskul ba chen po 'di skang gsungs//gsang ba bI bidag go//
7b7(da): khyod rje sngon gyi N+Da yale lam ci//'jig rten sdug bsngal ma shes sam//snying rje sa la bab nas kyang //'jig rten gdung ba gsal pa'i rigs//si so'i rigs kyi cha gzung nas//ma rurungs pa dag kun 'dul te//'di khrid thab suM tshigs rnams kyi//las
8a1(da): spyod na gzugs smras spyan / slo ga cugs ga dzungs gas/de m+WA phyu/ ma ma rgya la 'og/dum pa lajug lam
8a2(da): bo 'jig rten kun las rgyal ba'i mchog/gsang pa'i dgas 'dzin rdo can//dgyes shing gangan la banongs nas ni//cho 'drul chen po kun bstan nas//'jig rten log bI ga tso bo rnams//ska la dang ldan par lung bstan pa//las dang skal ba
8a3(da): ldan zhing //rgyal bar 'byung ba'i lung bstan pa'i//bsod nams chen po skal can rnams//sprul pas ji b+har lung bstan bi na nau// ltar dus la 'dir bab kyis//'khor ba'i snying por lung bstan phyir//cho 'phrul rnams kyi byin rlabs kyis//bstan pa'i snying po
8a4(da): ngas pa zhig/ji ltar ba shad cing lung bstan bzhin//skal ldan can la 'gro bar byo'o//drag shul can zhes bya 'i ri//dgon pa chen po'i gnas 'di ni//ye shes snying po can rnams kyi//theg chen gsang ba 'byung ba'i gnas//thog ma tha ba'i dus mang par//
8a5(da): brnams kyis bsngags pa yin//thog ma tha ma med pa'i//rgyal ba dpag tu meng ba yis//dam pa'i dgon glanas chen po dang//sems kyi rikyil 'khor me long bzhin//bstan pa de ltar bdag gis kyang //thog ma tha ma med pa ru//bskul ba'i dus ni ga
8a6(da): byung bar//bdag kyang de bzhin gyi 'phrul snang //zhes 'gros so//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongos gajus pa'i mngochen po las/gang gi gdung bu sel bar bsgos pa'i la'i sde byin pa'o///de nas de'i tshong stong //snong 'du
8a7(da): rgyal pos nga skad gsungs nas//mi gnas pa'i 'jig rten khams//gling bzhi pa las 'dzam gling gi/drag shul can zhes bya ba'i ri//gnod sbyin 'du ba'i sa yin te//chos 'dzin rigs kyi grub gnasu//thugs rjes la mngoni sprul gshegs/
8b1(da): sa rnams yongasu g.yos gyur te//rmang du byung ba'i cho 'phrul gyis//thams cad sbyangs te bde bar snang //sngon du gnas pa'i skal ldan can//dman pa'i lus 'dzin dam pa'i rigs//mi la rgyal rigs dram che//rgyal bu brgya byin ba lta su
8b2(da): shar de tshe dam pa'i rigs can rnams//dga' ba dang bcas the tsom skyes//spobs pa chen po'i dri bo dris//e mA ngo mtshar rmad rI che//skyes bu chen po khyod su zhig/gang nas byon te gang ltar byung //gang la rtan+da nas gang du b+h+yon//don gyi cher ka
8b3(da): ci nas dri//cho 'phrul chen pos 'di skad du//bani cis kyang mi bskyong ngo //mi 'khrug rdo rjesems pa yin//snying rjes g.yo zhing spyong pa na//sring po gsum gyi gnyen gcig pa'o//stong po chen po nga yi yul//rdo rdzogs chen pa'i gas
8b4(da): pa ni thabs mchog dpag tu med//ma ni shes rab brjod las 'das//thog ma med pa'i dus kyi tshe//nga'i yid bzhin nor bu stor//nor bu rin chen bdud rtsi de//yod par ma shes stor ma tshor//de tshe kun nas dbul ba dang //thar mang nad kyis
8b5(da): gzir gyur zhing //mun pa mun na glu smug du 'khyams//bgrod par bya ba'i lam yAng stor//gdus par 'gyur yang der ma shes//de tshe nga yi bu shes gnyen mchog/byams mgon dam pa zhig gis bstan//e ma'o khyod ci mi shes
8b6(da): sam//mthong yun kun du bde ba'i//de ni srid chen po 'byung 'gyur ba//nor bu rin chen ma rlag gam//'on de yod par mi shes sam//'di ci chad par 'dug ma tso//de skad bshes gnyen mchog nges spyan//de tshe nga yis slar blun
8b7(da): pa don srid chen po 'byung ba de/yod par mi shes stor mo tshor//'di dang phrad par bya ba'i thabs//bshes gnyen khyod kyis lung ston cig/dam pa de'i dmigs bstan pa//de dang myur du 'phrang 'dod na//btsal ba'i thabs la rtsim bya
9a1(da): klosas pa sa / snang ba/ rgyangs sa yod sgyus/ pa nga 'ams kyi spya / sa sa shi sa byung /mamachen gyur na pa /
9a2(da): rnams kyi khyim yin te// ni snga ma na 'gyur ba'o//nga de rnams kyi rnyeng gnas yin//de nid thos nas dmas//da la ba'i chang dbyed du//ngas pa la yim ltos te//blo gros ngas pasangs bshad nas//ga 6
9a3(da): / m+pu gnyis pa mas su TIg 'dun 'azu zhen sa pa/ gas sa rlo gas nga pa mas rgya ma du sa/sa nga pa lam thod //rgyan 'das pa'i grong nga pas/
9a4(da): nan dag tso gas/martul da pa da // nyid phyu ga tu sa na/ byung / ts+tsha smras ma//'ang rgyu nga/kun sa mdzud d+hA ba/sa spyo ba gas d+d+ha
9a5(da): sa pa snang lams pa shi /'di smra saphyi sa/ sa sa /
9a6(da): sa da pas/rlung gsum bsgral pa ngam panas kyis/mar par ring ba / ma pas kyi pa 'a /jos stong ba yig 'ang rdzas/mi ga pas sa par ngas sa kyis//gang nig yo da pa de shog na
9a7(da): kyi sman pa ste/grang byer gsum gyi ning pa rnams/gtso ning byungs bar rab bcas kyis/gzhig pa rgo pam dzi non/'dithams kyi mkhan te/bgrol bam log pam stong rnams/mnor dring ba ngas bcas kyis/yang dag pa ma 'do kyi
9b1(da): 'di gog/'ani thugs rje'i spya ras pa lan//'khor ba'i gyu bung 'jigs pa las//thams cad sgrubs par dram bcas kyis//'jigs dang la 'dod lam 'di shog/l-I rdo rdo rdo chen ste//'khor ba'i g.yul chen dpag dpung bas//'gro
9b2(da): dra sa dngas bcas kyis//gang zhig mkhar 'ang lam 'di shig/nga ni thabs kyi tshong dpon te//'dod ba rgya mchos mi ngoms rnams//gtom chen sbyin par dam brgyas kyis//gang shig gter 'do lam 'dir shog/nga ni thabs kyi sgron ma ste//mun dag
9b3(da): drug tu 'thams pa rnams//mun nag bsal bar rab bcas kyis//gang zhig snang 'jo la ma 'dzi shog/nga'i ying bzhin bus/gAng sa ming bzhin 'dod pa rnams//ying bzhin byung bar sa bcas kyis//rgyun gyis grub 'do bar 'dzi
9b4(da): shig Shi ni ga'i hUM mig ste//bka' snom tha rams gsang ba rnams//rnam par 'byung bar dam bcas kyis//gang zhig glo ba 'dod lam 'drir shog/nga ni ga/'i 'grel ba ste//gsung gi glegs pa ma zab mo rnams//nges par grol bar ma bcas kyis/
9b5(da): gang dag shes 'dod lam 'dir shog/rnam dag lam chen kun sgrol 'dir/skal ldan gang zhig su bzhugs pa//nor bu rin chen 'od 'bar ba//nges par 'char gyis brtson par gyis//de nyid sems kyi me long de//mngon sum gsal ba rtogs ma khang
9b6(da): 'gro ba'i lus la sangs rgyas yin//rdo rjegs chen grwas d+hi ba~M'aM//dus ngan dam pa'i mdzod ma rgyog nasas//'dir dgres gsal bar bshad myur bas//b+hU dang mi'i rigs 'dzin pas//rim par brgyad de grag smra par 'gyur//sgyu mi 'jig rten thams
9b7(da): tshun mi ma gas pas/sa ma kyim 'khor gapa mi gyur/dbyi du rdzun pa zhugs/rab ba byang ni las mjug nga pa'i grumang ba/ su mam buM/oM chu na
10a1(da): @#/bzhin//de skad sprul pa'i sgra byung bas//sngon du byung ba'i rigs can rnams//mchog tu dga' ba tshar skyes te//dga' mgu rangs pa'i bsam pa yis//lus btu dang shin tu gus sbyar nas//spyan sngar 'dug ste 'di skad gastsol
10a2(da): mtshan mo mun nag sprin rum nas//klog 'gyu bar snang ston lta bu re//thig ma tha ma med pa yi//de bzhin nyid kyi dbyings nyid las//rnam ngag lam tshan de nyid du//byung ba'i skyes bu don grub khyod//dam pa rnams kyi gsang 'dzin cing //gsang ba ba'i brang/
10a3(da): por grags//bka' rnams 'gro bar khyod bsngags pas/khyod ni bka' 'grel chen por grags//sems kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la//mdzod gsum gas nge ba'i nyid shir bswa//shaM sau byi'i mun pa kun gsal phyir//khyod ni gsal ba'i nyi mar
10a4(da): /grags//gdul dka' ma lus kun 'dul zhing //bdud kyi bdud de bdud rnams 'joms//'jigs pa'i tshogs kyang 'joms byed pas/khyod ni bdud kyi bdud tu grags//de bzhin nyid kyi ngo bo nyid//sems ni rang bzhin 'od gsal ba//chos kun gzug
10a5(da): brnyan snang bas na//chos kyi me long zhes kyang grags//dug 'joms sman gyi dkyil 'khor gyis//zug rngu 'byin pa bla na med//nad do cog gi dgrar gyur pa/khyod ni sman gyi rgyal por grags//dga' mgu rangs pa'i mdzad pas na/khyod ni gdung ba sel bar
10a6(da): grags//'dod pa yid bzhin bskong bas na/khyod ni yid bzhin nor bur grags//da mA pa rnams dang mthun mdzod la//bdag cag gi rig pa 'dzin rnams la//dam pa'i sbrul bA gang ltar nubya//dam pa'i sgron ma ji ltar bkod//dam pa'i gsang lung ji ltar byung /
10a7(da): gsang ba brgyud pa'i 'gro ba mdzod//dgongaso pa'i tshig gsum 'gro ba mdzod//nam pa'i rdo rje 'gro ba mdzod/ces gsol ba btab po//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgos pa 'dun pa'i mdo byin po pa las//lung ngas pa 'grol bar gsol ba btab pa'i le'u su
10b1(da): pA'o///de skad ces gsol pa las//gsang ba'i bdag pos bka' stsal pa//sngon du byung bar lung bstan pa'i//'gro ba lus kyi sems dpa' rnams//gdung sngags dri dang bcas par//sgyu ma'i rtog pa dgrol gyis nyon//sems
10b2(da): kyi dkyil 'khor de bzhin nyid//namakhar bzhin du nub pa med//rang bzhan ye nas 'od gras la bas//de la gang sgrib de nyid sel//tshad ma gnyis kyis gang gzhal ba/shes bying yang tshang mar 'gyur//nyes legs rtswa ba'ang de nyid pas//de nyid de
10b3(da): gyur ba med//sems kyi rang bzhin 'od gsal po//dkar bas dkar ba'i ni zla med//sangs rgyas mya ngan yongasu mi 'ang //chos kyang nub par mi 'gyur te//le lo n+dan la phan gdags phyir//sprul pa rnams ni 'anga' tshul ston//ji ltar namakha'i nag 'gyur na/
10b4(da): de tshe mya ngan 'dar bar 'gyur//gang dag gdug pa ma rungs pa//bdag med stong de'i stobs kyis 'dul//rang bzhin 'od gsal spros bre le ba'i//bstan pa rgya mtsho de nyid las//rang byung song gi rtsi la can gyis//kun tu 'bar zhing snang bar byed //bslab pa lhag pa'i
10b5(da): sems nyid pas//bsgrub pa kun gyi mchog 'di ni//sa rab snang ba phyis 'byung ba'i//'gro ba lus kyi sems dpas 'dzin//mther thug med pa'i sems can rnams//byang chub snying por ye gnas pas//de bzhin nyid du rgyud nges phyir//kun kyang mi slob de lung
10b6(da): dastan//sems nyid yod dang songo zhes//byis pa rnams ni brjid par byed//mkhas pa yi nas brjod dang dral//yod seng rtog pa'i tshig mi brjod//nga yi gsang sngags gsang ba 'di//sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang yin phyir//gang mi dpogs par gzhan pags/
10b7(da): la/gsung bar sgra gcig tha dad 'byung //dam pa rnams kyi gsang lung 'di//gang dag mi shes rmongs 'char bas//dug dang bdud rtsi rdzabs na//drang byed gnyis ka sreg par 'gyur//kun gsal me long ring ba che//ma 'dres yongs rdzogs ston pa de/mtha
11a1(da): ltar 'pho 'gyur meng bas na//'tsho ba'ang klog 'byung sbyin bzhin med //byang chub snying po yod pa'i bar//de sring bstan pa gnas pa yin//thog ma tha ma med pas nas//'thung bsna phyi yong ma yin//dang po mad las 'byung brjod pas/
11a2(da): thatham 'du stong bral med par 'gyur//gdung 'dzin thog ma'ang de bzhin nyid//tha ma rang de las gzhan mi 'gyur//'di nas de bzhin nyid 'dir gshegs//bangal khyab chen po'i zhing na bzhugs//sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'ang gsal bas//de bzhin nyid du rna/
11a3(da): snang byis pa'i ched du cho 'phrul zhig/me tog bkod pa'i 'jig rten na//ma grags pa zhes bya bar snang //snang ba nyid na snang ba med//de bzhin zhing khams gang dag du//sprul pa'i sangs rgyas snang mdzad pa//sgyu ma'i sprul pa ji bzhin du//mi snang 'on kyang
11a4(da): gzhan na snang //mi bskyod rdo rje thugs mchog kyang //thugs rje sprul pa byin rlabs kyis//'jig rten khams ni dpag mang du//nub snang rang byung rung zhi yin//skye med ye shes nyid kyi dbyings//gdod nas bder go rishegs snying po la//rnam rtog blo bur las kyis/
11a5(da): sgribs//de nyid 'gro mgon skyabs chen yin//rgyal ba'i dgongs pa tshig gsum po//'phrul gyi blo dang gos mchog ste//yun gyi don srid chen po bas//blo gros dam pa'i yul du snang //rgyal ba kun gyi thugs 'jug pa//ji bzhin pir de kho na//nyid du nges
11a6(da): pa'i ngo bo lan blo bzhag de nyid nges pa yin//chos kun nam pa'i mtshan nyid//ye shes sku nan rgyal ba rnams//sku la gangs bcas dngos meng par//sku gdung ring bsrel ji ltar blobyung //sems can rnams la phan don du//med pa nyid las sprul pa yin
11a7(da): zla ba mi srab cus 'dru ga/khrugs rna thangs kyis panag dul dbyan/ngas pa sod srol gang yang ba/khyim dag lags yong 'dra/gang ci drug btab ting 'bru yi ni//da bsreg yong bar gyur/gzugs snang drab sgogs snying
11b1(da): sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje gsum//mchod gnas yid rten dam pa zhes//'gro mgon rdo rje sems dpas bshang //dpag tu med pa'i rgyal ba rnams//'khor ba'i sems can rgyud las byung //'khor ba'i sems can rgyun chad na//sangs rgyas rgyun yang chad pa yin/
11b2(da): dper na na mkha'i 'ja' tsho ni ne//dang po gang nas 'ongs pa dang //tha ma gzhan du song ba med//de nyid rang byung rang zhi ltar//sprul pa rang byung rang zhi bas//de nyid de la nub pa med//'das kyang ma dral tha dang yin//ma 'das pa na gcig min te//bral dang ma bra
11b3(da): ga ba yong//des na gang li nI phyir gdung //gdung ba rmongs pas 'phrul pa yin//e ma'i ngo mtshar dzi b+ha ru//'dzin rje ltar 'khrul ni grwa//zhes gsang ba'i ba gre po ngas//sing ge rnam par d+ha nga'i tshul gyis gnas btab pas//rmongs pas gdungs
11b4(da): rnams la wa dga' mgu rangs nas gdungs pa yongasu sangs te/si mas ma bcos par gnas pas gnas te/bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad 'od gsal bar gyur to//de nas lang ka'i bdag pos/gsang ba'i bdag po la gsol pa/gsang ba ba'i bdag
11b5(da): ngo tshan go/gdung ba thams cad sil tA ma/'on te mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi gdung ba 'ba' zhig sel/gsang ba ba'i bdag pos smras pa/lang ki'i bdag po kho bo ni gdung ba thams cad sel ba te/'das pa'i dus kyi gdung ba ji snyed pa yang bsal to//da ltar gyi
11b6(da): mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i gdung ba yang bsal/'jig rten gyi khams gzhan rdul snyed kyi gdung ba yang bsal/mi 'ongs pa'i dus kyi gdung ba ji snyed pa sel bar 'gyuraro//de bas na gang dag yongasu gdung ba rnams ni mi shes pa rmongs pa/shed
11b7(da): ma bye ba kho na yin gyi/shes pa ma rmongs pa mkhas pa shed bye ba rnams ni/gang du yang gi drung ba mang pa/gdung ba dang bral ba yin zhes thos pas/lang ki'i bdag po la sogs pa lus kyi sems dpa' rnams/yong su gdung ba las sang so//sangs rgyas

12b4(da): /sangs rgya myangan pas 'das sa ga/shin te la gtan gyi khrims mtha' dgan zhing bams ji snyed pa'i stonam bgrod pa'i sangs rgyas mya ngan las ngas pararda gyur na dris pa lngas/gsang pa'i bdag pos sgyus gsang 'i bangag po pa rlom

12b6(da): brdung shig tsho'i phyin no//rag 'aras 'di ni sbrul ping sa sa rgyas cag gcig ga zhing 'ams yin par phyi //ngo bonan du bsam gyis mi sbyang ba'i sangs rgyas rnying ga/'jig rten gyi rnams na ga la dume na/snod mi bya nga ma /
12b7(da): dgang rdzab rtsog sprub pa sa su ma rdzu la ste bzhi du ltung //des paga'i mang songs byang dub mis pa ma yo tshan pa rdzos pa/gsang ba'i dag pa nga zhing 'a sem tho nag
13a1(da): /sprul pa'i sangs rgyas nya gcig mya ngan las 'das pa 'di/sangs rgyas gang gi sprul par yongasu bzung bar bgyi/gsang ba'i bdag pos smras pa yang gi'i bdag po 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa'i sprul pa bsam gyis//
13a2(da): /mi khyab pa las/sprul pa tshe gcig 'ba' shig kho nar zung shig/smras pa gsang ba ba'i bdag go zhing khams 'dir shAkya thub pa zhes grags na/'di ji ltar yongasu shes par bgyi zhes smras pa/lang ka'i bdag bo rdo rje sems
13a3(da): dpo'i sprul pa zla dwags 'da' zhig/lang ka'i bdag po de ci'i phyir zhe na/rdo rje sems dpa'i sprul pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas 'khor ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dpung gtan bcom pa'i phyir/thub pa chen po zhes
13a4(da): yongasu gragaso//ji ltar 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcur yang thub pa chen po zhes bya bar grags rje pa ni 'di lta ste/lha'i gon su thub pa chen po srid thub 'dzin zhes bya bAr grag so//lha ma yin gyi gon su thub pa chen po rnams par
13a5(da): rgyal ba zhes bya bar grag so//mi'i gnasu thub po chen po shAkya seng ge zhes bya bar grag so//byol song gi gnasu thub pa chen po rnam par gnon pa zhes bya bar grag so//yi dregs kyi gnasu thub pa chen po rnam par snang byed ces grags/
13a6(da): so dmyalta ba'i gan su thub pa chen po chos kyi rgyal po zhes bya bar grag so//kye lang ka'i bdag po ngar yang ma zad/de ji lta ba de ltar de bzhin du mi mjed kyi 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu na/thub pa drug gi sgron ma yongasu snang ba de lta de lta/
13a7(da): rdul snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i/'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu mtha' yas pa na/rdul snyed kyi thub pa drug gi sgron ma/mtha' yas par snang ba yang 'di lta ste/shar phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gling bzhi pa sto/
13b1(da): phrag bcu gnyis 'das pa na/'jig rten gyi khams dri ma med pa zhes bya ba'i zhing khams de thams cad rin po che sna tshogs las grub pa de na gnas pa'i 'gro ba rnams/tshe dang longs spyod thams cad sum cu rtsa gsum dang mnyam pa/bar ma dor
13b2(da): 'che ba med cing /rdzus nas skye ba sha stag kyang 'dod pa thams cad bsams pas 'grub ste/de ni sbyin pa yang med gtong ba yang med/len pa dang slong ba po yang med te/'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dag ni yang /gtong slong yod pa dag gautam
13b3(da): thos pan/de la yongs sngon pa~M yin+da te/don rdo rje sems dbyir sprul pa thub pa'i dbang po de/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa dri snyed sgra zhes bya byar bstan nas//theg pa chen po'i chos sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'od gsal ba/gsang sngags kyi ngo bong
13b4(da): nyid sgron ma chen po yongasu snango //de nyid kyi 'jig rten drug gi phyo gas bcun yang grub pa drug gi sgron ma snang bas/'gro ba drug gi sems kyi dkyil 'khor yongasu bsal bar bya'o//yang thub pa'i dbang po nga/lho phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams stong /
13b5(da): phrag brgyad 'das pa na/'jig rten gyi khams yon tan 'byung ba zhes bya ba na/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa dkon mchog sprin zhes bya bar sprul nas/sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'od gsal ba gsang sngags kyi ngo bo de nyid snang bar bya'o//de nyid kyi 'jig
13b6(da): rnyini drug gi phyogs bcu dag na yang /thub pa chen po drug gi sgo nas smyung bas/'gro ba drug gi sems kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar bye'o//yang thub pa'i dbang po nga nub phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams stong phrag nyi shu 'das pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgyan
13b7(da): shin tu ma pas gang cas rdzus nas skye sa gangan ste/tshe lo'i rgyam bri ba ni stong gnas pa'i nang 'ams ngan/dzin na gagasas pa shA thub ba yo na nga gnas nas bya bar bstan nas/sems kyi rtsom mkhor 'angags la pags 'jos
14a1(da): @#/kyi ngo bo de nyid ston to//de nyid 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu na/yang thub pa drug gi sgron ma snang bas/'gro ba drug gi sems kyi dkyil 'khor bsal bar bya //yang thub pa'i dbang po do byang phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams stong phrag
14a2(da): sum cu 'das pa na/'jig rten gyi khams bag tsha ba med pa ces bya ba na/dri bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub po seng ge sgra zhes bya bar bstan nas/sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'od gsal ba/gsang sngags kyi ngo bo de nyid ston to//de nyid kyi 'jig rten drug gi phyogs
14a3(da): bcu na yang /thub pa drug gi sgron ma snang bas/'gro ba drug gi sems kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar byed do//de bzhin du thub pa'i dbang po de shar lho'i 'jig rten gyi khams stong phrag brgya bcu rtsa bzhi 'das pa na yang /'jig rten gyi khams rnam par mdzes pa zla
14a4(da): bya ba ni de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa mdzes pa'i tig ces bya bar bstan nas/gong ma bzhin du chos ston to//lho nub mtshams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams stong phrag bdun bcu 'das pa na/'jig rten gyi khams rab tu snang ba zhes bya ba na/de bzhin gshegs pa
14a5(da): shAkya thub pa gzi brjid rgyan ces bya bar bstan nas/gong ma bzhin du chos ston to//nub byang mtshams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams stong phrag lnga bcu 'das pa nA/'jig rten gyi khams rab tu dang ba zhes bya ba na/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa de tsan dan dri mchog

16a1(da): @#/ba'i rgyan zhes bya ba'i zhing khams na/sangs rgyas me tog bkod pa'i rgyal po zhes bya bar bstan nas/yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du chos ston to//yang mdzes par snang ba zhes bya ba'i zhing khams na/sangs rgyas thams cad snang ba zhes bya bar

20b7(da): dpa'i de bzhin nyid//rdo rje sems dpa'i de bzhin nyid gang yin pa de ni de kho na nyid //gang chags pa med pa'i sems kyi de bzhin nyid ni/rdo rje sems dpa'i de bzhin nyidado//rdo rje sems dpa'i de bzhin nyid ni de kho na nyid//gang ni sdang gi sems kyi de bzhin nyid ni/rdo rje

24a6(da): bkun shes pa/gcig tu thub pa/mi 'byung ba/thams cad 'byung ba/zhi gnas pa/tha dad pa/gcig pa/mau gi yeng ba/kun tu g.yengs pa/sems dus gsum du mnyam par shes pa/sems de kho na par shes pa/ji lta rang gi sems shes pa
24a7(da): de ltar gzhan gyi sems kyang rab tu sheso//bdag gi sems ji ltar stong pa de ltar thams cad kyi sems kyang stong par rab tu she so//rang gi sems mnyam par shes pa/de bzhin du thams cad kyi sems kyang mnyam par rab tu she so//des chos thams cad
24b1(da): /mnyam par rab tu sheso//chos thams cad kyang mnyam pas byang chub kyang mnyam par rab tu she so//des chos thams cad kyi dbying su rab tu she so//chos thams cad kyi dbyings de ni kun gyi thun mong ma yino//zhes sems la gnas pa/sems la spyod pa/sems
24b2(da): la dbang ga sems la lta ba/sems mthong ba/sems ngo shes pa lags na/gsang ba ba'i bdag po ci'i phyir/dam pa 'ba' zhig gi spyod yul yin gyi/sa pa rnams kyis ma yin zhes smra/gsang ba pa'i bdag pos smras pa/lang ka'i bdag po de de lta yin
24b3(da): mod kyi/de dag ni thams cad nas thams cad du sems kyi ngo khon nyid la rgyu mtshan nyid 'brur zhig tu shes par zad kyi/'bras kyi nyid du yong su mi she so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta ste/gangs pa dang /sa ba rnams mi dra nga bzhi'i rgyur yongasu chud ba ste/
24b4(da): bzhi gang zhe na/gang dra ba chen po dang /dra ba dang /dra ba phyed dang /dra ba cig pa'o//de yang byis pa rnams dang /smyon pa rnams dang /'du shes chung ba rnams dang /'du shes cig pa rnams kho na ste/gangs pa rnams ni/sems rgyu 'ba' shig las/mi shes pa'i
24b5(da): phyir 'du shes cig pa ste/de dag ni dra bar chud pa/dra bas bcings pa/sha stag pas/'bras bu'i nges pa rnyed po shin tu dka' ba yin//de ci'i phyir zhe na/lang ka'i bdag po 'di ltar sems 'bras bu'i de kho na nyid gang yin pa de ni/sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang
24b6(da): gsung du med pa/rdo rje sems dpas kyang mtshon du med pa yin na/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis lta ci smos/yang lang ka'i bdag pos dris po/gsang ba pa'i bdag po ci khyod nyid kyis kyang sems 'bras bu'i de kho na nyid du nges pa da ma rnyed dam/de skad ces dris
24b7(da): ba lasan gsang ba pa'i bdag pos smras pa/lang ka'i bdag po/kho bos kyang da dung nges pa ma rnyed pa'i phyir/gzhan la bstan par rab tu pha'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/da ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa'i nyag cig gi dgongs pa'i spyod yul du gyur pa'o//zhes
25a1(da): @gsang ba'i bdag po ngas de skad ces smras pas/thams cad byang chub las 'khrul pa'i ye rdza ma yongasu chad par gyur to//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo chen po las/khyad par dam pa'i le'u ste/bcu pa'o//
25a2(da): /de nas lang gi'i bdag po la sogs pa/'gro ba lus kyi sems dpa' rnams ngo mtshor du gyur/ma du gyur te/'di skad ces smra so//kye gsang ba pa'i bdag po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyis bka' stsal pa ltar na/gsang ba pa'i bdag po
25a3(da): /ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bdag po rdo rje sems dpa'i gsang ba dam pa mi nor ba de kho na nyid/the tshom thams cad gcod pa lags na/'bras bu'i de kho na nyid gseng ba dam pa ston mi spyod pa ste/shin tu ngo mtshar che ba ltar na/bdag cag
25a4(da): gis nam nges pa rnyed par 'gyur zhigu/zhes smras pa las/gsang ba pa'i bdag pos smras pa/kye dman par lus kyi sems dpa' dgu/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi/mnga' bdag rdo rje sems dpa' nyid kyis kyang /gsang sngags kyi de kho na nyid ma rnyed
25a5(da): kyi bar du bskal pa grangs med gsum rnyed du dpag tu mang ba'i dka' ba dpyad kyang /rnam pa thams cad ma mkhyeno//de ji lta zhe na/ji lta ji ltar rgyud yong su dag pa'i sangs rgyas dpag tu med pa byung ba dang /'byung ba yang de kho na nyid kyis nges pa ma rnyed kyi bar du/
25a6(da): pa' ba spyod pa ste/'di ltar bskal pa bzang po 'di'i 'og tu mtha' khob kyi bskal pa rigs ngan drug bcu 'byung //de'i 'og tu bskal pa grags ldan zhes bya ba la sangs rgyas stong phrag bcu 'byung //de'i 'og tu rigs ngan gyi bskal pa stong phrag brgyad
25a7(da): nu 'byung //de'i 'og tu bskal pa skar ma bkram pa lta bu la sangs rgyas stong phrag brgyad cu 'byung //de'i 'og tu mun pa'i bskal pa sum brgya 'byung //ngo ro 'og tu bskal pa mdzes ldan bye ba la/sangs rgyas stong phrag brgyad bcu rtsa lnga 'byung //ji bzhin du//
25b1(da): mthar thug pa meng ba snron pa'i bskal ba dang /mun pa'i bskal brtag par rgyun gyi khor mo badran ma pa 'byu ngo //de lta na ngargal pa bzang po 'di nying la/sangs rgyas stong rtsa gnyis 'byung //brnal ba bzang po 'ji sngon du yang /mun pa'i bsngal ba brgya
25b2(da): byabo de'i sngon du bskal pa rdzogs ldan zhes bya ba la sangs rgyas bye ba phrag brgya byung //de 'das pa'i sngon du mtha' khob kyi bskal pa brgya byung //de 'das pa'i sngon du bskal pa bzang rgyan bya ba la/sangs rgyas bya ba phrag brgyad cu rtsa ban byung ngo //de 'angs
25b3(da): pa'i sngon du mun pa'i bskal pa lnga brgya byung //de 'das pa'i sngon du bskal pa mthong dga' zhes bya ba la/sangs rgyas bya ba phrag bcu byung //de 'das pa'i sngon du mun pa'i bskal pa bdun brgya byung //de 'das pa'i sngon du bskal pa de ga' ba zhes bya ba la/sangs
25b4(da): stong phrag drug cu byu //de la sogs par mtha' yas par mun pa dang /sgron mi'i bskal pa 'dren mar thog ma dang tha ma med par byung zhing 'byung ste/gang sgron ma ji snyed pa byung zhing 'byung ba rnams kyang gsang sngags kyi nges pa de kho na nyid ma rnyed kyi bar du/
25b5(da): dpag tu med pa'i dka' ba dpyad kyang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen mi mkhyen mkhyen par mi 'gyur ro//yang gsang sngags kyi nges pa de kho na nyid rnyed ma thag por rnam pa thams cad mkhyen to//mkhyen par 'gyur ro//de bzhin du sngon gyi bskal pa grangs med pa
25b6(da): grangs med pa las kyang gangs med par 'das pa/mang po che do med pa/bsam gyi mi khyab pa na/bskal pa chen po mngon par dga' ba zhes bya ba la/sangs rgyas khri sum stong byung ba'i dus na/thog ma'i sangs rgyas kun tu bzang po zhes bya ba'i bstan pa la
25b7(da): rgyal ba'i sa dang /bram ze'i rigs dang /rjer rigs dang /dmangs rigs dang /gdol pa'i rigs dang lnga las/mchog chen po lnga byung ba ni/'di lta ste/khye'u ba skyong zhes bya ba dang /khye'u dra 'i ta zhes bya ba dang /khyi ru bas byin zhes bya ba dang /khye'u las
26a1(da): byin nas bya dang /khyer dum ngur nas byang byung ngo //de'i tshang ngas na sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' mi byang byang skyo ces bya'o//de'i tshang dusum/sangs rgyas tshematha' yas mi khyed d+ha li Sh+Tags bya'o//de'i tshang 'i dus na/sa sa rgyas
26a2(da): rim chen 'byung ldan ni/khye'u dbas sbyin zhes bya'o//de'i tshe nga'i dus na/sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad ni khye'u las byin zhes bya'o//de'i tshe ji dus na/sangs rgyas don yod grub pa ni/khye'u dum bu ra zhes bya'o//de'i tshe ngo'i dus na/khye'u nga rnams kyis sangs
26a3(da): rgyas kun tu bzang po de la rnal 'byor mchog tu sems bskyed pa'i dus/'jig rten gyi skyabs ni/sems brgyad du skyed cing 'jig rten pi'i lam brgyud nas 'grod de/'gyur dbang ma nges pa'i sa par gnas//de'i tshe sangs rgyas sum khri sum stong la the tshom bca
26a4(da): kyang nges pa ma rnye //las dang ba'i bskabs na/sems gsum bskyed cing /mtha' bcu chub nas/rten pas gzhi 'o sa par gnas//de'i tshe de'i dus na/sangs rgyas bzhi khri bzhi stong la the tshom bcad kyang nges ma rnyed //rin chen 'byed pa'i skabasu sems bzhi
26a5(da): rdzogs te/mthar bcu chung nas/bsod rnams gtar bar gyur to//de 'i chos sa rgyas b+h+rUM khri lnga stong sa the tshom bcad kyang nges pa ma rnyed//yang thugs chen 'byin pa'i bska su sems cig rdzogs te/mthar bcu chu ba nas/'jigs pa lnga las 'as/
26a6(da): te rkye nas byung bar 'gro ba ris par gan so//nga'i tshis sa rgyas drug drug stong paji chom bcad pas phyogs tsam shes te drugs phyin par gyur to//de yang 'di ltar/sgyum spyong ba'i skyes bu de 'khor sa bskyor de'i mabyang du/rin d+han pa su
26a7(da): tshogs kyis/rabang bsgyur thabs kyi theg pa ston/yang lung chen thod pa'i snying /sems bdun rdzogs te/lhan cig gnas pa'i drim dag pas/ri bo las nal te/dmigs pas 'bras du skye ba'is par gan no//ngori mi sangs rgyas bdun khri bdun stong gi
26b1(da): nga ga tsun can mgan//ngas pa pa sa ng+ya pa pa sa la ba/sa rmyi na na /nga sa sbyid byu sh+tsi m+b+ha nA rgya dus mchos b+h+yo/ chen rdzoms pa'i gru /
26b2(da): gsum bskyed cing mtha' drug chub nas/gnas pas mi 'gyur ba'i sa par gan sa//de tshe sangs rgyas brgyad zhi brgyad stong las lung bstan pa thob nas/gang du gyur to//de yang 'di ltar ngan rgyal tshab skyes bu /chos kyi rgyal po gang da'i/gdung 'tsho ba dgar
26b3(da): ldan thes tshon ston/mi gnas bcu dus cho 'phrul snang /lang ka'i bdag po de ltar na/lus dang po pa'i rigs kyi bu de dag ni//rigs lhar skye ba blangs pa de nas kyang bskal pa dpag tu med pa brjod pa las/'das pa/thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i dus na
26b4(da): sangs rgyas lnga li byang chub mchog tu sems bskyod nis/'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyal po rnam par snang byed kyi bu lngar skyes pa ni/'di lta ste/mdzes pa'i me tog dang /bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dang /nor sbyin 'dzin dang /zla mdzes dpal dang /kun la mdzes
26b5(da): pa'i dzi sa ba d+hAr g+hur to//de ga las kyi da po 'dun pa'i nas khyor bar gyur pa nyid la yid la byung ba zhes bya ba thob bo//des nam par la 'gro ba'i ltas mthong bar gyur to//de nas rigs chem phyung ba 'i la 'byor bar gyur pa nyid le/'gro du
26b6(da): gcig pa'i ting 'dzin pa'o//nges bdag nyid yi ge'i 'phreng bar sna ba'i lhas mthong bar gyur to//de nas dbugs chen phyin pa'i rnal 'byor par snang ba nyid la/yongasu mi ldog pa'i ting 'dzin thob bo//nges 'jig rten gyi khams nor bu rin po ches gang
26b7(da): bar sbyong ba'i lhas mthong bar gyur to//de nas lung chen thob pa'i rnal 'byor du snang ba nyid la/de bzhin nyid kyi ting 'dzin thob bo/ngos seng ge la zhon te/thams cad zil gyis gnon pa'i lhas mthong bar gyur to//de nas rtsal chen rdzogs pa'i rnal
27a1(da): byor du snang ba nyed pa/kun tu snang ba'i ting 'dzin thob ste/des nyi zla 'khor lo bskor ba/thams cad 'o gyis gsal bar byang ba'i lhas mthong bar gyur to//lang 'i bdag por byar gyur zi kyang /gsang sngags kyi ngob chon/
27a2(da): das babyung ba nim yi //de tsi byi m+b+ha la 'i sag blo gang sas dang po po mas klung ga rgyal chu rgyas dran gyur bam rgyas drangs tha rnams pa d-iD+ga nga /skyes drangs pa po sa na /skye zungs pa chas dang /smras
27a3(da): dam po las kyi rdo rje dang /skyes bu dam po rdo rje dbyungs zhes bya bar ning khams thams cad du grag so//del lar ni rgya la tsha ba skyes bu zhes byibar brjo cing du ltar grug so//la lo ra ni sring ba tham pa zhes bya bar brjod cing grag so//la la ni mtshan
27a4(da): po'i sangs rgyas zhes bya bar brjod cing grags kyang /sangs rgyas kyi dgongs pa gsang ba ngas pa/da dung nyam su ma myong ste/mnyamo pa la 'jog dgos pa'i phyir//de ci'i phyir zhe na/skyes bu dam pa de dag gis nam mnyam pa la ma bzhag pa'i bar du
27a5(da): sangs rgyas kyi dgongs pa nyam su ma myong nas/yang dag pa'i rtogs pas dang po'i sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' la gsol pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams la ni/mnyam par bzhag pa dang ma bzhag pa mi mnga' bar rnam pa thams cad mkhyen/
27a6(da): na/bdag cag gi bskul pa dpag tu med par/dka' ba dpag tu med par bgyi ba thams cad rdzogs par dgyis kyang /mnyam par ma bzhag par du/sangs rgyas kyi dgongs pa myong bar ma gyur pa 'di/ngo mtshar che la gA s+ho//bang bar gshegs po
27a7(da): 'dri mche ma lag so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ljin sa rdo rje sems dpa'i snying pongs paka stsal pa/kye skyes bungs pa nams/gang de 'i 'ani gshegs pa nyid spyan gyis grub par 'dod ba rnams kyis na/sangs rgyas kyi chos tham/
27b1(da): cad nas thams cad du/yong su rdzogs par bya ba gsum las/khyed rnams kyis gang bya ba yong su rdzogs par byas/bsrung ba yong su dag par byas na su zin kyang /da dung blo gros kun tu chub par ma bya'o//de bas ma gdong nas dam pa rnams kyi de kho na nyid la blo gros
27b2(da): kun tu chub par gyis shig dang /de yong su chub par bzhin du/ngom pa rnams kyi dgongs pa myong bar 'gyuraro//de skad ces bkar stsal ba las/skyes bu dam pa rnams kyis gsol pa/oM mo 'o bcom ldan 'das/bcom ldan 'das kyis ni/bdag cag
27b3(da): rnams la shin tu ngo mtshar rmang du gyur pa/dpag par dka' ba/the tshom skye ba/som nyid mtshal bar bka' stsal na/blo gros kun tu chub par baM gyid pa'i dam pa rnams kyis de kho na nyid ces bgyi ba gang lags bshad du gsol/bde bar gshegs pa
27b4(da): bag du gsol zhes gsol ba las/sangs rgyas kun gyi bcom ldan 'das gsong sngags kyi ngo bo nga kho ni nying ngas bka' stsal pa/kyai skyes bu dam pa rnams/dam pa rnams kyi de kho na nyid 'di ni/thabs che la sgo yangs/tshigs chung la don che/thag
27b5(da): la brtag dka'/nyen che la bsrung dka' ba'i phyir/ngas kyang khyed la phyin ci ma log par bstan par rab tu dka'o//de skad ces bko' stsal pa dang /skyes bu dam pa de rnams rmag chad par duso//shin tu rab tu ngus nas/gsang ba'i sgo dbye bar gyur
27b6(da): te/gsang sngags kyi dngos po brgyad du yongasu shes par gyur to//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa chen po'i mdo chen po las/rgyud dag pa'i spyi rgyud bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa
27b7(da): a kyi ma malas 'a rjes kyi 'dzus ngas ra rdzog skyes lus pa de dag pa di rna sa yul ga skye ru rje mas ma rdzums ga bsngal gyi gtubs stsa c-ig kya 'i gyi lus sa/
28a1(da): /nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' de kho na nyid la/skyes bu dam pa de rnams kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das yod ji ltar mi ngu bar bgyi/bdag cag gis
28a2(da): btsal ba ci chad par ma gyur ram/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis blo gros ji ltar bgyi/bdag cag rnams kyis bshes gnyen dmu pa mdzad gsol/bdag cag gis dpag tu med pa ji snyed cig tu/dka' ba dpag tu med pa spyad nas yongasu chub
28a3(da): par 'dod na/da dung gsang sngags kyi ngo bo de kho na nyid/ming tsam yang thos pa'i bskal ba ma mchis na/yong ji ltar bis lags/de bzhin gshe gas pa thams cad kyi bcom ldan 'das/rdo rje sems dpa' de kho na nyid nges bka' stsal pa/skyes bu
28a4(da): dam pa rnams de skad ma zer cig/gsang sngags ni kun gyi thun mong ma yin te/don de kho na nyid ni/sangs rgyas nyag gcig gi dgongs pa dam pa sgrar med pa/rtog dpyod thams cad dang bral ba/'di zhes mtshon nas du med pa/brtag pa dka'i zhing bstan
28a5(da): par dka' la/brjod pa'i tha snyad rnams dang bral ba/dpa' dang bral/mkhas kyang sman du yod pa ma yin pa/tshad med pa/tshad dang bral ba/tshad mi 'am/gtan tshigs ma/dpags pas mi dpogs pa ste/khyed kyis ji ltar me shes ma thos pa de ltar
28a6(da): gang rgyud yong su dag pa'i sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis kyang /da dung gsang sngags kyis ngo bo de kho na nyid mngon par ma thob/mngon par mu rtogs/mngon par rdzogs par sangs ma rgyaso//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gsang sngags kyi de
28a7(da): de zhing rnam par yong su dag pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' zhes mi rimigs shing brtsal du meng ba las/sa bskul gnas pa'i skyes bu dam pa rnams kyis ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po gsang sngags kyi ngo bo de kho na nyid nges pa da
28b1(da): la yod par ma shes te/chos nyid du rdzogs cing /dpyod pa dang ma bral/sangs rgyas rnams ni snying po byang chub la mi rtogs pa'i ngang du mi g.yo bar gnas te/mngon par 'tshang mi rgyu bar gnas na/khyed ci'i phyir ngu bar byed/de skad ces bka' stsal pas/skyes bu dam pa/
28b2(da): /de rnams dga' mgu rangs te/dbugs phyin nas 'di snyam du sem so//ji ltar sngon du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis kyang /da dung gsang sngags kyi ngo bo de kho na nyid mngon tu ma gyur na/bdag cag la sogs pas lta ci smos/yang
28b3(da): /ston pa rdo rje sems dpa' de kho na nyid 'di ni/sangs rgyas kun gyi dge ba'i be shes gnyen dam pa nyid kho na bas/bdag cag gi blo gros 'di la chub par gor ma tshig snya mo//de nas skyes bu dam pa de rnams kyis/bcom ldan 'das/de kho na nyid la 'di skad ces
28b4(da): /gsol to//bcom ldan 'das gsang sngags dam pa de kho na nyid/gang yongasu brjod pa las 'das te/gsung bar mi mnga' bas bstan cing brtag par rab tu dkara' ba lags zhe na/'on kyang bcom ldan 'das nyid ni/blo gros chen po rnams kyi yul du nges shing
28b5(da): the tshom thams cad gcod pa dam pa rnams kyi dam pa nyid lags pas na/bdag cag gi blo gros dam pa nges par chub pa dam pa'i phyogs/dgongs pa dam pa mnyam pa nyid du bstan du gsol/zhes gsol ba las/bcom ldan 'das de kho ni nyid nges
28b6(da): bka' stsa la pa/skyes bu dam pa rnams leg so//de so//'di lta ste/gsang sngags dam pA da kho na nyid de ni/dpyod pa thams cad dang bral ba/mtha' dang dbus mi dmigs/sgro bskur med pa/blang dor med pa/rgyu 'bras las 'das pa/ye nas dag pA/ye nas
28b7(da): zin pa bya ba byed go bskul ba med ga bsgrub tu mengadag bcol du med pa/gzhan nas mi 'byung ba/gnyen po meng pa/byed pa po ming ga ma byas pa/ma btags pa/bdog ming ga ma bcis pa/ma smang gau nga yin pa/nges pa/ma nor ga/ji bzhin pa
29a1(da): ma 'gags pa ma skyes pa/mi nub ga mi rlag pa mi sna ga ma byung ga mi 'gro ba/ye nas gnas pa/kun la khyab pa/ming med pa/gzugs med ga/mtshan ma med pa/tha snang ma ga ltar med ga bzung du ma dag/mtshon du med pa
29a2(da): dpape' mngag/tshad med pa/brtag tu med pa//rtog ge las 'das pa/dpag tu med pa/bsam du med pa/bsam gyis mi byab pa/kun gyi 'byung gnas/thams cad nas thams cad du btang snyoms chen po/stong pa nyid rang rig parang byung ba kho na'o//de
29a3(da): skad ces bka' stsal pas/skyes bu dam pa rnams chos thams cad blang dor med par chud de/ngo mchir skyes nas the tshom du gyur to//e ma'o//bdag cag gis dam pa rnams las sngon du thos pa ltar na/sngon gyi dam pa rnams ni/byang chub blang ngo //nyon mongs
29a4(da): pa dor ro/rnga nga ni blo gros yongasu chub pa'o//zhes thos nA/skyes bu chen po 'di ni nyon mongs pa dang /byang chub la gnyisu myeng par blang dor me //zhes gsungs pas/ngo mtshar to//snyam du cang mi smra bar gnaso//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' de kho na nyid
29a5(da): des skyes bu dam pa rnams kyis yongasu rtog pa brtsal ba'i ched du yang gsungs pa//skyes bu dam pa rnams gang khyed kyi tshom yongasu za ba na/nyon mongs pa'i rang bzhin gang yin gang las byung /byang chub kyi rang bzhin gang yin gang las byung
29a6(da): nges par rtogs shig dang /gang yong su mthong ba nyid na the tshom chad par 'gyuraro//de skad ces gsungs pas/skyes bu dam pa de rnams kyis nyon mongs pa dang /byang chub kyi rang bzhin gang yin/gang las byung ba rtags na/rang bzhin stong par mthong /rtsa ba sam/
29a7(da): las byung bar mthong /sems nyid ma byung ma skyes skye ba mang ba/ye ji bzhin pa/rang bzhin lhun gyis grub pa/rtsa ba byang chub kyi sems dam pa/'o na nyid nges par gud /sangs rgyas kyi dgongs pa nyam su myong ba ni/'di lta ste/sems dang sems las byung ba
29b1(da): thams cad na mkha' lta bur gyur to//mnyam par bzhag pa dang ma bzhag pa thams cad 'das te/tha mir dbugs phyin nas/yang dag pa'i nges pa yongasu ngo //rnyed par gyur to//kun tu rab tu rnyed nas/yang skyes bu dam pa de rnams rmag chad
29b2(da): bar du so//yang rdo rje sems dpa' de kho na nyid des mkhyen bzhin du/skyes bu dam pa de rnams la bka' stsal pa/kye skyes bu dam pa khyed ci'i phyir du//de dag gis gsol pa/bshes gnyen dam pa 'di lta ste/lam gyi nang na nye lam pa/thabs
29b3(da): kyi nang na dam pa 'di/sngon du ma thos bskal ba chung /e ma'o//ngo mtshor rmad kyi chos/yul gyi don srid 'di lta bu/don rag gzhan la ma lus par/gzhan la re rtsol e'o 'khrul/bya ba'i las lang sha thang dang nyid du gral/de nyid chen pa lhun

61a5(da): nyon cig zer ba las kyang ngam nyan/nga khyod kyi dbang du bdag la dzin/gosu se mo dor bzod la rang gi dka' thub la bang bar mnos shig ces bskur nas/bdud kyi dngos po bzhis yongasu gzhams pas/gtsang ma rigs kyi 'phags pa rtog
61a6(da): pa la zhugs nas phyir log//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis yang sangs rgyas pad+mo'i gdung dang sde'i sprul pa kun tu spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug li yongasu bsgo ba/rgyal ba kun gyi chos sku pad+mo'i gdung /chos rje 'od dpag med pa
61a7(da): /rdo rje chos/spyan ras gzigs sprul hri las rngam chen ston/ngag gi dbang phyug rta gdong zhal gyis gnon//zhes yong su bskul bas//sprul pa rdo rje rta gdong chen pos/dur khrod kyi bdag po chen po'i gnasu sdang bar mdzad nas/ha ha
61b1(da): pU lu hu lu hU/lung lhung nor tser rta skad drag pa'i sgra d+mang+yas byung ba las/srin po zlum yongasu daphyu zhum zhing khrag labums pa tsam du gyur yang /dur khrod kyi bdag po khon ngo bo rlung gi da N+Dal gtum skad du/ru tra ma tra ma rat+na nes brgyugs
61b2(da): nas/rnam par brjid phyung ste/'gyangs pa las zil gyis ma non te/slar kho na ngo bo des slar bsgo ga/mgrin bu sngon yang khyod lta bus bdag ma non pas/khyod kyis bdag rngan pa ma yin te/nyid kyi gnas gang yin par mnos shig/ces
61b3(da): tha'i kha brdab pa byas te/yang bdud kyi dngos po gzhis yongasu gzhams pas/gtsang ma'i ris kyi 'phags pa rtag pa smyos nas/phyir log snyam pa las/de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha' lta bu la gcing sme mi mnga' nas/ma log par/slar
61b4(da): hen pa las/kho na ngo bo des thim pa rngams nas/de bzhin gshegs pa dbang phyug nges/'thun 'jug tu kho na dga' ba ltar thim+b+ha hu ha no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa des/dbu kho na'i gtsug tu phyung /zhabs kho na'i gtsug tu phyung /rdo rje hU~M wA
61b5(da): rgyas pa ru kun tu rgyus te/kho na des ma bzod pa'i nga ro 'o dod kyi sgra mthar ston pa ni a tsA'o//ci mdzad myur bar mdzod cig ston//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa des/kho nA de sgrib pa sbyangs pa mdze badra bar kho na'i gtsug tu byung ste/nam mkha' la rta'i
61b6(da): dbyangs phyung bas/srin po kho na'i tshogs bur mer thor shig tu gyur te/zil gyis non cing bag khums nas gsol pa/dpa' bo chen po'i 'bangasu mchi/ji ltar bgyi ba bka' stsal cig/zhes zhus pas/de bzhin gshegs pa des
61b7(da): gsungs pa/ngas ni khyod ma btul gyis/khyod kyi gnyen po rdo rje hU~M zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis dbang gis dbang bskur ba shin tu gtum rngam 'khrugs pa ma lus shing 'i bzad pa/bdud kyi bdud chen po 'jigs byed 'dang
61'a1(da): zhig byung gis/de la skyabasu dong cig ces gsungs te/chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang du mi dmigs par gshegs so//de nas gsang ba 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis/'dus pa chen po rnam par
61'a2(da): bgros nas/'phrin las bzhi'i sgo gang gis 'dul ba'i skabs la bab brtags na/gang ni rgyus kyi gdul bya mi 'dra nas/dbang drag gis gdul bar bka' ba gros dam par nges par bcad pa'i mdo/yongasu gsungs pa ni 'di lta ste/
61'a3(da): de bzhin gshegs pa rnam grol spyod pa'i tshogs kyis bka' bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa ni/'di lta ste/shin tu 'gog pas zhi ba la/khros pas phan par mi 'gyur te/rnal 'byor ma bcos de nyid las/zhi bar de bzhin gshegs kun mdzes
61'a4(da): rnal 'byor tshul can zhi bas kyang /srid pa'i myu gu 'gog nus na/rnal 'byor yong mang stong pa'i mthu sa/'gog pa bslangs pa smos ci dgos/shin tu bsngol skams brlan pa la/gang du 'gog rnams bslang ba'i phyir/'gag med stong pa'i
61'a5(da): chos nyid las/sna tshogs mi 'gog ji bzhin me dzo /zhes nges pa dam par bca //de nas de bzhin gshegs pa ngo bo nyid meng pa'i tshogs kyis bka' bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu stong pa rgyun ched la/dngos pos phan par mi 'gyur
61'a6(da): te rgyun gyi byang chub stong nyid las/ming 'gyur de bzhin gshegs kun mdzod/stong nyid tshul can stong pas kyang /ci yang med pas gnas 'gyur na/med pa las byung rnal 'byor mthus/mthar zang mu zlog smos ci dgos/shin tu rgyun ched
61'a7(da): bzlog pa dang /gang du meng las skyes pa'i phyir/rgyun dang bcas pa'i stong nyid las/che yin rgyu ni mi 'chad par mdzod/ces nges pa dam par bcad//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa ngo bo nyid mi 'gyur ba'i tshogs kyis bka' bcad pa'i mi ngo gsung
61'b1(da): pa shin tu thar zug brtag pa la/med pas phan par mi 'gyur te/snying po byang chub de nyid las/bdag nyid chen por thams cad mdzod/mi 'gyur tshul can brtag pas kyang /dngos po brtan g.yor byang nus na/bdag nyid chen po lhun grub mthus
61'b2(da): /ther zug bzlog pa smos ci dgos/bdag tu zhen pa bzlog pa dang /dngos 'dzin bka' bsgo bsgyur ba'i phyir/bdag med brtag pa'i chos nyid las/byed med rang bzhin nyid du mdzod/ces nges pa dam par bcing //de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje'i
61'b3(da): gdung dang sde'i tshogs kyis bka' bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu gdug cing gtum pa la/zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te/shes rab thabs kyi ngo bo las/khro bor de bzhin gshegs kun mdzod/khro bo'i tshul can khros pas kyang /'jig
61'b4(da): rten gsum ngeg skrag nus na/sangs rgyas thugs rje khros pa yis/khams gsum ma lus smos ci dgos/gdul dka' ma lus 'dul ba/'a dang /dam tshig la sdang rnam sbyang phyir/'bar ba chen po'i gar dgu las/dur khrod gar gyi phyag rgyar mdzod
61'b5(da): ces nges pa dam par bcad//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje'i snying po de kho na nyid dang nges pa zhes bya ba'i tshogs kyis bka' bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu log par zhen pa la/bden pas phan par mi 'gyur te/gsang chen sbyor
61'b6(da): bya'i ldem dgongs lnga/thams cad 'thun 'jug nyid du mdzod/mdzad spyod tshul can spyad pas kyang /thams cad bka' bzlog byed nus na/thugs rje'i 'thun 'jug mdzad spyod kyis/lam log drung srong smos ci dgos/dngos pos rdzo
61'b7(da): bo grol ba dang /dug gis dug nad bsal ba'i phyir/thabs mchog dpag gis mi lang ba'i/mdzad pa gar gyi phyag rgyar mdzod/ces nges pa dam par bcad//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa/nor bu'i gdung dang sde'i tshogs kyis bka'
62a1(da): bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu 'jungs shing brnams pa la/btang bas phan par mi 'gyur te/mang la mi zad kun 'byung wa/thams cad mi zad nor bur mdzod/dkar thub tshul can 'gags pas kyang
62a2(da): gcig 'tsho bar byed nus na/med la 'dod dgur 'byung ba yi/khams gsum gdung ba smos ci dgos/'jungs pa tshim par bya ba dang /yong su gdung ba gsal ba'i phyir/theg chen gsang ba'i longs spyod las rnam khar/
62a3(da): mdzod kyi phyag rgyar mdzod/ces nges pa dam par bcang //mi g.yo'i tshul can gnas pas kyang /dam pa'i las lam btsod byung ma bya bral mi g.yo bde chen po/'jig rten g.yengs pa smos ci dgos/mi lom los rgyung bcang /
62a4(da): ba dang /lo lo g.yeng ba'i dgra bzhom phyir/btang snyoms dal 'byor chen po las/bya med gnas par phyag rgyar mdzod/zhes nges pa dam par bcadado//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa glang po'i gdung dang sde'i tshogs kyis bkas bcad
62a5(da): pi'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu gti mug rmongs pa la/bstan pas go bar mi 'gyur to/khyad par cir yang mtshungs pa las/thams cad gti mug chen por mdzod/gti mug tshul can rmongs pas kyang /thams cad mun par byed nus na
62a6(da): mi 'byed blang dor med pa yis/khams gsum rmongs pa smos ci dgos/mun pa'i skye mchod gzhom pa dang /rmongs pa na mkhar bya ba'i phyir/gti mug rnam dag ye shes las/ma 'dres yengs rdzogs phyag rgyar mdzod//zhes nges pa dam par
62a7(da): brjod/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa pad+mo'i gdudus dang sde'i tshogs kyis bka' bcang pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu chags shing zhon pa la/gnyen pos phan par mi 'gyur te/tshigs rab chen po'i ye shes las/thams cad chags pa
62b1(da): chen por mdzod/chags pa'i tshul can dpyad pas kyang /khams gsum khrag tu byed nus na/'dod chags rnam dag chags spyad pas/khams gsum zhen chags smos ci dgos/'dod chags khrag mtsho skams pa dang /chags pas
62b2(da): chags pa sbyang ba'i phyir/'dod chags rnam dag ye shes las/'dod chags gar gyi phyag rgyar mdzod/ces nges pa dam par btso do//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bkas bcad pa'i mdo gsungs pa/shin tu dngos por zhen pa la/
62b3(da): bkag pas phan par mi 'gyur te/shes rab thabs kyi ngo bo las/thams cad 'thun 'jug spyod pa mdzad/mthun 'jug tshul can cha 'thun pas/khams gsum log par byed nus na/thugs rje gar gyi mdzad spyod kyis/'jig rten blo log
62b4(da): smos ci dgos/de la sogs pa mtha' yas rnyed/'jig rten 'jig rten mtha' yas kun/'dul ba'i thabs ni sna tshogs kyis/grangs te yang dag gsang bar sbyor//khro la byams dang khro tshul mdzad/zhi la zhi ba rlung sbyor nyid/drag shul can la
62b5(da): drag po nyid g.yol g.yem dang gnyen por mdzad/chags dang chags la rnam slong nyid/g.yeng la sems bsdung giyeng ba nyid/rmongs la khyab par mnyam pa nyid /ri la ting 'dzin ri bo nyid /phrag dog can la phrag dge tshul/de la sogs pa mtha'//
62b6(da): yas rnyed/kun zhi mnyam dang mi mnyam pa'i/na mkha' dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa/khyad par 'dod pa'i lus can gyi/kun gzhi kun gyi dbyings na dpal/rdo rje snying po rab tu gnas/sems can sangs rgyas kun gyi dbyings/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin
62b7(da): nyid ye nas yin par nges pas na/mtha' thug med pa'i sems can rnams/ji ltar dga' ba 'dod pa bzhin/ma bkag ma bcos ji bzhin par/nges pa nyid du gyur pa yis/thabs mchog dpag gis mi long ba'i//mdzad skyong gar thab sbyin
63a1(da): dpung gis/ji ltar sbyar ba de nyid las/ma g.yos ma gol phyings na dbug/nyid du nges pa'i ngo bo lnga/mthar yas dpag tu mang ba'i/sgo nas thams cad ma dkag par/ci dga' nyid du bde grangs te/dam pa rnams kyis nges
63a2(da): par mdzod/'dir ni nan tan drag shul gyi/mi zad 'bar ba'i ye shes las/dur khrod bdag po rab 'jigs pa'i/cha mchog 'bar ba'i phyag rgyar mdzod//ces nges pa dam par bcad///sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo
63a3(da): chen po las/bka' bgros dam pa bcad pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu lnga pa'o////lang ka'i bdag po de'i tshe de'i dus na/khro bo 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur pa zhig gis/shes bzhin du gang yongasu sang ba'i phyir/yang 'khor dang 'khod par
63a4(da): gyur pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bskul ba'i go cha blo gros thabs ldan zhes bya ba zhig la yongasu dris pa/kye sems dpa' chen po yi dgas chen po 'di/rgyu ni gang las byung /rkyen ni ci las gyur/'bras bu ni cir smin/rigs ni gar gtogs/las skal
63a5(da): gyi spyod pa ni ci spyod/ma btul ba ni ci nyes/btul na ci dgos/de skad ces dris pa las/bskul ba'i go cha chen po des smras pa/sems dpa' chen po gsang ba'i bdag go/yi dgas chen po 'di/gang legs pa'i rgyu rkyen las ni byung /nyes pa 'bar
63a6(da): zhig gi rgyu rkyen los byung ste/rgyu ni mun pa'i ri bo las byung //rkyen ni bka' go log pa'i dka' thub drag po las byung ngo //'bras bu ni sdug bsngal gyi ri mor smin to//rigs ni dman rigs 'khor ba/gdol pa 'byung po yi ngag su gtog so//las skal
63a7(da): gyi spyod ba ni/'khor ba'i dngos po ban la 'jol tog du spyong /'di ma gtul na/lus can gyi byang chub kyi snying po ring du nub pa'i skyon du 'gyuraro//btul na ni kun gyi snying po 'od kyi dkyil 'khor mngon du nye bar 'char bar 'gyuraro//zhes smras pa dang /
63b1(da): kho bos de la 'di skad ces smraso//e ma'o sems dpa' chen po/'o na dus 'di ni dus la bab bo//las ni las kyi mcho go/gsang ba 'di ni gsang ba'i yang gsang dam pa'o//thul kun tu rab tu thul+la/dus la bab pa'i skabs chen 'dir lan/
63b2(da): la bab pa'i sems can 'di/stongs la bab pa'i thugs rje yis/'du shes sbyar ba thugs rje'i mchog/stong gsum ma lus ston pa pas/'bum gyi char yang mi phong //zhes nye bar bskul to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad
63b3(da): kyi ngan phag gnas gyur pa/skyes bu dam pa rdo rje 'dzin tu snang ba la yongasu sgo ba mdzad pa/rgyal ba kun gyi gsang mdzod pa/thugs kyi bdag po rdo rje 'dzin/drag po brtul shugs chen po can/dpa' bo 'jig rten gsum las rgyal/
63b4(da): sngon gyi go chu chen po'i mthus/sangs rgyas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/gdul dka' dag po 'dul mdzad pa/bdud kyi bdud de bdud rnams 'joms/sems dpa' 'bar ba sngon po'i sku/'jig su rung ba'i dpa' byad tsan+da/gtumA rnga ma 'khrug
63b5(da): ba'i ngo bo yis/dur khrod bdag po rab tu choms/zhes yongasu bskul bas/skyes bu rdo rje 'dzin pa de/lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten 'di/'byung po'i rgyal pos dbang du byas/shin tu gtum rngam 'khrug pa des/lha mi'i 'jig rten
63b6(da): brlag ma dang /bzhin dang mngangs kyang mi gsal bar/kun tu mi ge mog por gyur nas/dus ma yin pa'i mun pa yang /mun nag smag du gyur pa'i 'thibs/tshe dang grangs pa'i nang dang rims/mi zad sna tshogs kun tu rgyas/rlung
63b7(da): 'brug dang ser ba dang /rnag khrag rdo dang mtshon cha'i char/dus ma yin par kun tu byung /skye min 'tsho'ang log par gyur/gdug pa sna tshogs sems can gyis/ shin tu nyams thag gdungs gyur pa'i/mgon ming pa yi 'jig rten
64a1(da): @#/de mkhyen des thugs rjes gdung ba ni/rab tu rngam pa'i brtul shugs kyis/rdo rje chos kyi rta gdong la/rdo rje'i tshig tu 'di skad du/ngag gi dbang phyug rdo rje gdong /lang ka'i yul gyi grong brdal gyi/dbus kyi ri bo ma la yA
64a2(da): rkorka thang dmar rgya mtsho'i ngo //dag shul yang ba'i rtse la dang /rdo rje thabs kyis lta ba'i yul/dur khrod bdagu po'i tshogs dang bcas/mings sgyu ma ling chen po le/khams gsum rdo rje bya sgron thong /cis kyang mi skyong mi 'khrugs pa'i/sems dpa'
64a3(da): sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin nyid//rdo rje snying po srid gsum mgon/stong pa chen po'i nam mkha' la/'bar ba chen po'i gar dgu mdzod/thugs rjes la gshegs nas kyang /mthun 'jug byis pa'i spyod pa mdzod/med la kun
64a4(da): 'byung stong nyid las/ji bzhin pa yi rgyal ba dang /stug po bkod pa'i zhing khams na/rnam dag lam gshegs rgyal ba rnams/bdag ni bka' las mkhan te/bka'i las kyi dus byung bas/sangs rgyas kun gyi gdul dka' ba/de dag ma lus bdag
64a5(da): gis 'dul/ma grol/yang bdag gis sgrol/ma bsgral ba yang dag gis bsgral/dbugs ma phyung ba dbugs phyung ste/yang dag nyid du dgod par bgyi/dgongs pa gsang ba kun 'dus la/rmad byung gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi/mtha' thug med par
64a6(da): sems can rnams/snying po bskul ba'i byin brlabs mdzod/ces rdo rje 'dzin pa des drag tu bsgral bdes/chos kyi dbyings kyi po drang nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad ye shes chen po'i me dus mthi'i bskal ba'i me ltar 'bar ba'i thugs rje
64a7(da): sprin dpung nam lir mtha' bo kla sa por 'ithabs nas/'bar ba chen po'i garare dgur mngon spyod du mdzad nas/rdo rje d+ha'i tshogs la bkas bsgo ba/song kun tu rab tu song /rta'i rdo rje stabs dgu yis/'jig rten blo gu'i 'dod pa snyom/'jag rtenanas/
64b1(da): po gtum bye tshogs/ngur khrong bskyams ldings spyod pa las/rdo rje rtogs pa'i bla gyon gyi/'khor ba ma bsgral bar du nga /dam bcas go ma gyon pa yi/snying rje chen po'i rtogs 'jug gis/'khor bar so mthara' tshong bar gyis/zhes drag tu/
64b2(da): bsgo bas/rdo rje chos rab bka' rakho lo/gzigaso dang rdo rje spyan rab ste/rdo rje pad+ma rang dga' bar/snang mdzad ngag gi dbang phyug tshogs byang chub chen po cang shis gtso/stabs chen dgu'i rdzu 'phrul gyi//rdo rje rta gdod sprul pa'i tshogs/nam la
64b3(da): khams kun yongs bkang nas/mA la ya'i rtse la lhags/'khor ba ma stong par du ni/thugs rje'i dbang gis spyan ras gzigs/stug po bkod pa'i rgyal ba yang /dgongs pa bsang ba cig tu 'dus/khams gsum yongs sgrol snying por dgongs dug
64b4(da): gsum stan dkrugs byin rlabs mdzod/'khor ba chos nyid spyan ras gzigs/sdug bsngal byang chub la mo du mdzad/las nyi derang byung ye shes 'brel/rnam smin sun byung rdo rje nyid/yi nas ji bzhin ma bcos pa'o//rang byung dkyil 'khor
64b5(da): chen por dgongs/gsang bdag rdo rje 'dzin pa yang /sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje las/sbrul po'i sras mchog rdo rje 'dzin/gzhon nu bye ba sa yar mdzad//thams cad dbyar gyi sprin dpung bzhin//na mkha' dbyings kyi mtha' yas par//ma la ya
64b6(da): rtse la lhags//'thun 'jug thabs kyi chu byang du//gang 'dir byis pa'i spyod pa yis//bdud kyi spyod yul kun mdzad de//'jig rten lam su byis par bstan/sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa 'dus pa'i mdo chen po las/las bzhi rdo rje bsgos
64b7(da): kye le'u ste/nyi shu drug pa'o///de nas 'jug 'thun skyes bu dri//'thun po'i sprin gyis gang gzigs na//las kyi yi dgas drag po des//dngos po bzhis ni dang byas pa//rnam par smin pa'i dngos po ni//bam re thong
65a1(da): ma ste za khrag dang ba shang m+b+hig tsham du//rkang drung byung ba glang mya nga //rnag dang chu snar la sogs pa de//'das kyi phru ma yongasu bskor//'bu shin tu bu chen po//sgru ma sbyang rgong bang ba dang //smra
65a2(da): sdig pa sdom la sogs//kang ka khwa skags 'ag pa'i tshogs//lU spyang stag seng la sogs pa'i//bdug pa can gyi 'khor gyis bskor//lha'i deng dpon ka ru Na'i//bu mo chung ma ma sring dang //'jig rten dbang phyug lha tshon gyi//ma sring bu mo chung ma
65a3(da): rnams//phrogs nas dbang du byas pa yis//smas dang byi mo bran mo'i tshul//bcu gnyis dang ni brgya de la sogs//ma mo phra men+da gnod sbyin mo//srin po sha za khrag 'phrog ma//bdud kyi mi 'dzin bla rkun ma//blon mo dmag dpon ste dpon ma//rung
65a4(da): dpon po nyi mngag par bcas//rang rang stan dang lag tshor bcas//ci bi gyi bkar nyan bka' stong 'khor//spong stong rngam pa'i tshul du 'khod//srid pa'i bu mo dri za brgyad//seng stag wa sbyang gi zhid tshogs dang //dur bya bya rig 'ug pa'i tshogs
65a5(da): bstod cing rngam pa'i tshul du 'khod//srin po'i nye gnas chen mo brgyud//ke ru ri rtsi'u ri bu mo ha//pe ta li dang bu ka si//kasma ri dang sma sha ni//tsan da li la sogs pa yi//gtum zhing rngam po'i mtshogs ma/shin tu 'jigs pa'i gzugs
65a6(da): 'khong /ngur khrong sir sgo bsrung ban//bergyan mo gdong mo gar log mo//de bzhin 'jug mo chen mo bani//gsang sgro 'dzin cing bka' sgo bsrung /dur khrod bdag pa'i gru 'degs ma//banam ma shin tu banam ma dang //ri rom dang khrag 'thung ma
65a7(da): chags pas byis pa'i mangs kyis 'khong//pama'i thong ba brtsegs pa'i dbus//gnas lpags 'ar ba'i gzim chen gyis/brtan par bta ba pa'i nang bdag so//nyi gnas kro ti shwa li la//khrong stsogs khrag gis bkang ba dang //'drum rjerdzas /
